y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CemuCR1oa65P1Vmt7mQC2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EWmFqdk7Ppj8YRfwmbjGET","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/cahn-hilliard_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r3/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp","content":"// Cahn-Hilliard phase separation benchmark.\n//\n// Hybrid parallelization: MPI (1D domain decomposition along Z, one rank per GPU)\n// + CUDA (all stencil work on the device, halo exchange overlapped with interior\n// compute) + OpenMP (host-side initialization/validation/gather post-processing).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                 \\\n    do {                                                                                                 \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                 \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                       \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err_));      \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                \\\n        }                                                                                                \\\n    } while (0)\n\nnamespace {\n\n// Thread block shape for the (x,y) tile; each thread sweeps a range of z planes.\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\n\nstruct Params {\n    int nx;\n    int ny;\n    int nzg;   // global grid size in z\n    int nzl;   // local (per-rank) number of z planes\n    int z0;    // global z index of the first local plane\n    double inv_dx2;\n    double inv_dy2;\n    double inv_dz2;\n    double gamma;\n    double e_AA;\n    double e_BB;\n    double e_AB;\n    double dtD;\n};\n\n// Local fields are stored with one ghost plane on each side in z:\n// plane 0 = lower ghost, planes 1..nzl = owned, plane nzl+1 = upper ghost.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const int z, const int nx, const int ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny));\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) chemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p, const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pc = c + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    // Rolling registers in z: the neighbour planes are reused across iterations.\n    double ccur = pc[base];\n    double cprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pc[base - plane] : ccur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double cnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pc[base + plane] : ccur;\n\n        const double cxx = (pc[row + xp] + pc[row + xn] - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double cyy = (pc[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pc[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * ccur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double czz = (cnext + cprev - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base] =\n            4.5 * ((ccur + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (ccur - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * ccur * p.e_AB) + 3.0 * ccur +\n            ccur * ccur * ccur - p.gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cprev = ccur;\n        ccur = cnext;\n        pc += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p,\n                                                                 const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pm = mu + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    double mcur = pm[base];\n    double mprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pm[base - plane] : mcur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double mnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pm[base + plane] : mcur;\n\n        const double mxx = (pm[row + xp] + pm[row + xn] - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double myy = (pm[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pm[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * mcur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mnext + mprev - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + p.dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mprev = mcur;\n        mcur = mnext;\n        pm += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Same pseudo-random field as the reference implementation, evaluated from the global index.\n__global__ void initKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const int nx, const int ny, const int nzl, const int z0,\n                           const unsigned long long vol) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int zl = blockIdx.z + 1;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || zl > nzl) return;\n\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(z0 + zl - 1) *\n                                             (static_cast<unsigned long long>(nx) * ny) +\n                                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[planeOffset(zl, nx, ny) + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nint g_rank = 0;\nint g_size = 1;\nbool g_cudaAwareMPI = false;\n\n// Exchange one ghost plane in each z direction. Called after the field is ready on\n// the device; the MPI progress overlaps with interior kernels on the compute stream.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const Params& p, const int down, const int up, MPI_Comm comm,\n                   cudaStream_t haloStream, double* h_send, double* h_recv) {\n    if (down == MPI_PROC_NULL && up == MPI_PROC_NULL) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_lo = d_field + plane;                                   // first owned plane\n    double* d_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl) * plane;      // last owned plane\n    double* d_ghost_lo = d_field;                                     // lower ghost\n    double* d_ghost_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl + 1) * plane;\n\n    MPI_Request req[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n\n    if (g_cudaAwareMPI) {\n        if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Staged through pinned host memory: [0] = lower plane, [1] = upper plane.\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send, d_lo, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send + plane, d_hi, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_lo, h_recv, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_hi, h_recv + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const                                                                 \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                       \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err_));      \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                \\\n        }                                                                                                \\\n    } while (0)\n\nnamespace {\n\n// Thread block shape for the (x,y) tile; each thread sweeps a range of z planes.\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\n\nstruct Params {\n    int nx;\n    int ny;\n    int nzg;   // global grid size in z\n    int nzl;   // local (per-rank) number of z planes\n    int z0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double cnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pc[base + plane] : ccur;\n\n        const double cxx = (pc[row + xp] + pc[row + xn] - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double cyy = (pc[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pc[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * ccur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double czz = (cnext + cprev - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base] =\n            4.5 * ((ccur + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (ccur - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * ccur * p.e_AB) + 3.0 * ccur +\n            ccur * ccur * ccur - p.gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cprev = ccur;\n        ccur = cnext;\n        pc += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p,\n                                                                 const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pm = mu + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    double mcur = pm[base];\n    double mprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pm[base - plane] : mcur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double mnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pm[base + plane] : mcur;\n\n        const double mxx = (pm[row + xp] + pm[row + xn] - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double myy = (pm[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pm[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * mcur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mnext + mprev - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + p.dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mprev = mcur;\n        mcur = mnext;\n        pm += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Same pseudo-random field as the reference implementation, evaluated from the global index.\n__global__ void initKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const int nx, const int ny, const int nzl, const int z0,\n                           const unsigned long long vol) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int zl = blockIdx.z + 1;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || zl > nzl) return;\n\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(z0 + zl - 1) *\n                                             (static_cast<unsigned long long>(nx) * ny) +\n                                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[planeOffset(zl, nx, ny) + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nint g_rank = 0;\nint g_size = 1;\nbool g_cudaAwareMPI = false;\n\n// Exchange one ghost plane in each z direction. Called after the field is ready on\n// the device; the MPI progress overlaps with interior kernels on the compute stream.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const Params& p, const int down, const int up, MPI_Comm comm,\n                   cudaStream_t haloStream, double* h_send, double* h_recv) {\n    if (down == MPI_PROC_NULL && up == MPI_PROC_NULL) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_lo = d_field + plane;                                   // first owned plane\n    double* d_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl) * plane;      // last owned plane\n    double* d_ghost_lo = d_field;                                     // lower ghost\n    double* d_ghost_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl + 1) * plane;\n\n    MPI_Request req[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n\n    if (g_cudaAwareMPI) {\n        if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Staged through pinned host memory: [0] = lower plane, [1] = upper plane.\n    if (down <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions\ndouble computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {\n    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;\n    \n    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);\n    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);\n    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - \n                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);\n    \n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const double cv = c[idx];\n                \n                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) \n                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,\n                        const std::vector<double>& mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    for (size_t z + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const each thread sweeps a range of z planes.\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 8;\n\nstruct Params {\n    int nx;\n    int ny;\n    int nzg;   // global grid size in z\n    int nzl;   // local (per-rank) number of z planes\n    int z0;    // global z index of the first local plane\n    double inv_dx2;\n    double inv_dy2;\n    double inv_dz2;\n    double gamma;\n    double e_AA;\n    double e_BB;\n    double e_AB;\n    double dtD;\n};\n\n// Local fields are stored with one ghost plane on each side in z:\n// plane 0 = lower ghost, planes 1..nzl = owned, plane nzl+1 = upper ghost.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const int z, const int nx, const int ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny));\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) chemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p, const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pc = c + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    // Rolling registers in z: the neighbour planes are reused across iterations.\n    double ccur = pc[base];\n    double cprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pc[base - plane] : ccur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double cnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pc[base + plane] : ccur;\n\n        const double cxx = (pc[row + xp] + pc[row + xn] - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double cyy = (pc[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pc[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * ccur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double czz = (cnext + cprev - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base] =\n            4.5 * ((ccur + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (ccur - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * ccur * p.e_AB) + 3.0 * ccur +\n            ccur * ccur * ccur - p.gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cprev = ccur;\n        ccur = cnext;\n        pc += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p,\n                                                                 const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pm = mu + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    double mcur = pm[base];\n    double mprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pm[base - plane] : mcur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double mnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pm[base + plane] : mcur;\n\n        const double mxx = (pm[row + xp] + pm[row + xn] - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double myy = (pm[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pm[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * mcur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mnext + mprev - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + p.dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mprev = mcur;\n        mcur = mnext;\n        pm += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Same pseudo-random field as the reference implementation, evaluated from the global index.\n__global__ void initKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const int nx, const int ny, const int nzl, const int z0,\n                           const unsigned long long vol) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int zl = blockIdx.z + 1;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || zl > nzl) return;\n\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(z0 + zl - 1) *\n                                             (static_cast<unsigned long long>(nx) * ny) +\n                                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[planeOffset(zl, nx, ny) + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nint g_rank = 0;\nint g_size = 1;\nbool g_cudaAwareMPI = false;\n\n// Exchange one ghost plane in each z direction. Called after the field is ready on\n// the device; the MPI progress overlaps with interior kernels on the compute stream.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const Params& p, const int down, const int up, MPI_Comm comm,\n                   cudaStream_t haloStream, double* h_send, double* h_recv) {\n    if (down == MPI_PROC_NULL && up == MPI_PROC_NULL) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_lo = d_field + plane;                                   // first owned plane\n    double* d_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl) * plane;      // last owned plane\n    double* d_ghost_lo = d_field;                                     // lower ghost\n    double* d_ghost_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl + 1) * plane;\n\n    MPI_Request req[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n\n    if (g_cudaAwareMPI) {\n        if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Staged through pinned host memory: [0] = lower plane, [1] = upper plane.\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send, d_lo, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send + plane, d_hi, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_lo, h_recv, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_hi, h_recv + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    int bad = 0;\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) reduction(| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = 1;\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (g_rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (g_rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (g_rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", node-local rank.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, double* d_mu = nullptr;\n    double* h_send = nullptr;\n    double* h_recv = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t computeStream = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t haloStream = nullptr;\n    cudaEvent_t evCold = nullptr;\n    cudaEvent_t evMu = nullptr;\n\n    if (g_rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n\n    if (simComm != MPI_COMM_NULL) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cold, These are compile-time constants (as in the reference code) so\n// that the device code can fold them; FP64 throughput is the limiting resource here.\nconstexpr double DX = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DY = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DZ = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\nconstexpr double E_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double E_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double E_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double GAMMA = 0.5;\nconstexpr double DIFF = 1.0;\n\nconstexpr double INV_DX2 = 1.0 / (DX * DX);\nconstexpr double INV_DY2 = 1.0 / (DY * DY);\nconstexpr double INV_DZ2 = 1.0 / (DZ * DZ);\n\nstruct Params {\n    int nx;\n    int ny;\n    int nzg;   // global grid size in z\n    int nzl;   // local (per-rank) number of z planes\n    int z0;    // global z index of the first local plane\n};\n\n// Local fields are stored with one ghost plane on each side in z:\n// plane 0 = lower ghost, planes 1..nzl = owned, plane nzl+1 = upper ghost.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const int z, const int nx, const int ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny));\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) chemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p, const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pc = c + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    // Rolling registers in z: the neighbour planes are reused across iterations.\n    double ccur = pc[base];\n    double cprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pc[base - plane] : ccur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double cnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pc[base + plane] : ccur;\n\n        const double cxx = (pc[row + xp] + pc[row + xn] - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double cyy = (pc[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pc[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * ccur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double czz = (cnext + cprev - 2.0 * ccur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        mu[planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base] =\n            4.5 * ((ccur + 1.0) * p.e_AA + (ccur - 1.0) * p.e_BB - 2.0 * ccur * p.e_AB) + 3.0 * ccur +\n            ccur * ccur * ccur - p.gamma * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cprev = ccur;\n        ccur = cnext;\n        pc += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p,\n                                                                 const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pm = mu + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    double mcur = pm[base];\n    double mprev = (p.z0 + zbeg - 1 > 0) ? pm[base - plane] : mcur;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const int gz = p.z0 + zbeg + i - 1;\n        const double mnext = (gz < p.nzg - 1) ? pm[base + plane] : mcur;\n\n        const double mxx = (pm[row + xp] + pm[row + xn] - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dx2;\n        const double myy = (pm[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pm[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * mcur) *\n                           p.inv_dy2;\n        const double mzz = (mnext + mprev - 2.0 * mcur) * p.inv_dz2;\n\n        const size_t o = planeOffset(zbeg + i, p.nx, p.ny) + base;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + p.dtD * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mprev = mcur;\n        mcur = mnext;\n        pm += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Same pseudo-random field as the reference implementation, evaluated from the global index.\n__global__ void initKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const int nx, const int ny, const int nzl, const int z0,\n                           const unsigned long long vol) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int zl = blockIdx.z + 1;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || zl > nzl) return;\n\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(z0 + zl - 1) *\n                                             (static_cast<unsigned long long>(nx) * ny) +\n                                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[planeOffset(zl, nx, ny) + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nint g_rank = 0;\nint g_size = 1;\nbool g_cudaAwareMPI = false;\n\n// Exchange one ghost plane in each z direction. Called after the field is ready on\n// the device; the MPI progress overlaps with interior kernels on the compute stream.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const Params& p, const int down, const int up, MPI_Comm comm,\n                   cudaStream_t haloStream, double* h_send, double* h_recv) {\n    if (down == MPI_PROC_NULL && up == MPI_PROC_NULL) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_lo = d_field + plane;                                   // first owned plane\n    double* d_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl) * plane;      // last owned plane\n    double* d_ghost_lo = d_field;                                     // lower ghost\n    double* d_ghost_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl + 1) * plane;\n\n    MPI_Request req[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n\n    if (g_cudaAwareMPI) {\n        if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Staged through pinned host memory: [0] = lower plane, [1] = upper plane.\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send, d_lo, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send + plane, d_hi, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_lo, h_recv, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_hi, h_recv + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    int bad = 0;\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) reduction(| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = 1;\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", These are compile-time constants (as in the reference code) so\n// that the device code can fold them; FP64 throughput is the limiting resource here.\nconstexpr double DX = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DY = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DZ = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\nconstexpr double E_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double E_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double E_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double GAMMA = 0.5;\nconstexpr double DIFF = 1.0;\n\nconstexpr double INV_DX2 = 1.0 / (DX * DX);\nconstexpr double INV_DY2 = 1.0 / (DY * DY);\nconstexpr double INV_DZ2 = 1.0 / (DZ * DZ);\n\nstruct Params {\n    int nx;\n    int ny;\n    int nzg;   // global grid size in z\n    int nzl;   // local (per-rank) number of z planes\n    int z0;    // global z index of the first local plane\n};\n\n// Local fields are stored with one ghost plane on each side in z:\n// plane 0 = lower ghost, planes 1..nzl = owned, plane nzl+1 = upper ghost.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const int z, const int nx, const int ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny));\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) chemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p, const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? pc[base - plane] : ccur;\n\n    // The +z clamp can only apply to the very last plane of the sweep, so it is kept\n    // out of the hot loop.\n    const bool clampHi = (p.z0 + zbeg + zcount - 2) == (p.nzg - 1);\n    const int nfull = clampHi ? Called after the field is ready on\n// the device; the MPI progress overlaps with interior kernels on the compute stream.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const Params& p, const int down, const comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_lo, h_recv, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_hi, h_recv + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    int bad = 0;\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) reduction(| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = 1;\n        minVal = printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", each thread sweeps a column of z\n//            planes keeping the z-neighbours in registers.\n//   OpenMP - host-side O(N) work (result validation on the gathered field).","originalFile":"// Cahn-Hilliard phase separation benchmark.\n//\n// Hybrid parallelization: MPI (1D domain decomposition along Z, one rank per GPU)\n// + CUDA (all stencil work on the device, halo exchange overlapped with interior\n// compute) + OpenMP (host-side initialization/validation/gather post-processing).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#if __has_include(<mpi-ext.h>)\n#include <mpi-ext.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                 \\\n    do {                                                                                                 \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                 \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                       \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(err_));      \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                \\\n        }                                                                                                \\\n    } while (0)\n\nnamespace {\n\n// Thread block shape for the (x,y) tile; each thread sweeps a range of z planes.\nconstexpr int BLOCK_X = 32;\nconstexpr int BLOCK_Y = 4;\n\n// Physical parameters. These are compile-time constants (as in the reference code) so\n// that the device code can fold them; FP64 throughput is the limiting resource here.\nconstexpr double DX = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DY = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DZ = 1.0;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\nconstexpr double E_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double E_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double E_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\nconstexpr double GAMMA = 0.5;\nconstexpr double DIFF = 1.0;\n\nconstexpr double INV_DX2 = 1.0 / (DX * DX);\nconstexpr double INV_DY2 = 1.0 / (DY * DY);\nconstexpr double INV_DZ2 = 1.0 / (DZ * DZ);\n\nstruct Params {\n    int nx;\n    int ny;\n    int nzg;   // global grid size in z\n    int nzl;   // local (per-rank) number of z planes\n    int z0;    // global z index of the first local plane\n};\n\n// Local fields are stored with one ghost plane on each side in z:\n// plane 0 = lower ghost, planes 1..nzl = owned, plane nzl+1 = upper ghost.\n__device__ __forceinline__ size_t planeOffset(const int z, const int nx, const int ny) {\n    return static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny));\n}\n\n// Chemical potential: mu = 4.5*((c+1)e_AA + (c-1)e_BB - 2c e_AB) + 3c + c^3 - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) chemicalPotentialKernel(\n    const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p, const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pc = c + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n    double* pmu = mu + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n\n    // Rolling registers in z: the neighbour planes are reused across iterations.\n    double ccur = pc[base];\n    double cprev = (p.z0 + zbeg > 1) ? pc[base - plane] : ccur;\n\n    // The +z clamp can only apply to the very last plane of the sweep, so it is kept\n    // out of the hot loop.\n    const bool clampHi = (p.z0 + zbeg + zcount - 2) == (p.nzg - 1);\n    const int nfull = clampHi ? zcount - 1 : zcount;\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const double cnext = (i < nfull) ? pc[base + plane] : ccur;\n\n        const double cxx = (pc[row + xp] + pc[row + xn] - 2.0 * ccur) * INV_DX2;\n        const double cyy = (pc[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pc[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * ccur) *\n                           INV_DY2;\n        const double czz = (cnext + cprev - 2.0 * ccur) * INV_DZ2;\n\n        pmu[base] = 4.5 * ((ccur + 1.0) * E_AA + (ccur - 1.0) * E_BB - 2.0 * ccur * E_AB) + 3.0 * ccur +\n                    ccur * ccur * ccur - GAMMA * (cxx + cyy + czz);\n\n        cprev = ccur;\n        ccur = cnext;\n        pc += plane;\n        pmu += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update: cnew = cold + dt*D*lap(mu)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_X* BLOCK_Y) updateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                                                 const double* __restrict__ mu, const Params p,\n                                                                 const int zbeg, const int zcount) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= p.nx || y >= p.ny) return;\n\n    const int xp = (x < p.nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yp = (y < p.ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(y) * p.nx;\n    const size_t base = row + x;\n\n    const double* pm = mu + planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny);\n    const size_t off0 = planeOffset(zbeg, p.nx, p.ny) + base;\n\n    double mcur = pm[base];\n    double mprev = (p.z0 + zbeg > 1) ? pm[base - plane] : mcur;\n\n    const bool clampHi = (p.z0 + zbeg + zcount - 2) == (p.nzg - 1);\n    const int nfull = clampHi ? zcount - 1 : zcount;\n\n#pragma unroll 4\n    for (int i = 0; i < zcount; ++i) {\n        const double mnext = (i < nfull) ? pm[base + plane] : mcur;\n\n        const double mxx = (pm[row + xp] + pm[row + xn] - 2.0 * mcur) * INV_DX2;\n        const double myy = (pm[static_cast<size_t>(yp) * p.nx + x] + pm[static_cast<size_t>(yn) * p.nx + x] -\n                            2.0 * mcur) *\n                           INV_DY2;\n        const double mzz = (mnext + mprev - 2.0 * mcur) * INV_DZ2;\n\n        const size_t o = off0 + static_cast<size_t>(i) * plane;\n        cnew[o] = cold[o] + (DT * DIFF) * (mxx + myy + mzz);\n\n        mprev = mcur;\n        mcur = mnext;\n        pm += plane;\n    }\n}\n\n// Same pseudo-random field as the reference implementation, evaluated from the global index.\n__global__ void initKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const int nx, const int ny, const int nzl, const int z0,\n                           const unsigned long long vol) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_X + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_Y + threadIdx.y;\n    const int zl = blockIdx.z + 1;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || zl > nzl) return;\n\n    const unsigned long long linear_id = static_cast<unsigned long long>(z0 + zl - 1) *\n                                             (static_cast<unsigned long long>(nx) * ny) +\n                                         static_cast<unsigned long long>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((linear_id + 1ull) * 1299709ull) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[planeOffset(zl, nx, ny) + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nint g_rank = 0;\nint g_size = 1;\nbool g_cudaAwareMPI = false;\n\n// Exchange one ghost plane in each z direction. Called after the field is ready on\n// the device; the MPI progress overlaps with interior kernels on the compute stream.\nvoid exchangeHalos(double* d_field, const Params& p, const int down, const int up, MPI_Comm comm,\n                   cudaStream_t haloStream, double* h_send, double* h_recv) {\n    if (down == MPI_PROC_NULL && up == MPI_PROC_NULL) return;\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(p.nx) * static_cast<size_t>(p.ny);\n    const size_t bytes = plane * sizeof(double);\n    double* d_lo = d_field + plane;                                   // first owned plane\n    double* d_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl) * plane;      // last owned plane\n    double* d_ghost_lo = d_field;                                     // lower ghost\n    double* d_ghost_hi = d_field + static_cast<size_t>(p.nzl + 1) * plane;\n\n    MPI_Request req[4];\n    int nreq = 0;\n\n    if (g_cudaAwareMPI) {\n        if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_lo, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n            MPI_Irecv(d_ghost_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n            MPI_Isend(d_hi, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        }\n        MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Staged through pinned host memory: [0] = lower plane, [1] = upper plane.\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send, d_lo, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_send + plane, d_hi, bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, down, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL) {\n        MPI_Irecv(h_recv + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 1, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n        MPI_Isend(h_send + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, up, 0, comm, &req[nreq++]);\n    }\n    MPI_Waitall(nreq, req, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n    if (down != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_lo, h_recv, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    if (up != MPI_PROC_NULL)\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_ghost_hi, h_recv + plane, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, haloStream));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(haloStream));\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    int bad = 0;\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n\n#pragma omp parallel for reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal) reduction(| : bad) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const double val = c[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) bad = 1;\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &g_size);\n\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (g_rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (g_rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    if (g_rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n        printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Bind each rank to a GPU based on its node-local rank.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, g_rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int localRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        if (g_rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, \"No The benchmark is now parallelized with a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA scheme, unconditionally enabled.\n\n**Design** (`cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp`):\n- **MPI** — 1D domain decomposition along Z, one rank per GPU (device picked from the node-local rank). Each rank keeps one ghost plane on each side; the two per-step halo exchanges (`c` before the chemical-potential pass, `mu` before the update pass) are non-blocking and use CUDA-aware MPI when the runtime reports it, otherwise stage through pinned host memory. Ranks that get no planes (more ranks than Z planes) are split out into an idle set so the run stays correct. Results are gathered to rank 0 with a plane-sized MPI datatype (avoids 32-bit count overflow).\n- **CUDA** — both stencil passes run on the device. Each thread owns an (x,y) column and sweeps z, carrying the z-neighbours in registers so only one new plane is loaded per step; global-boundary clamping is hoisted out of the hot loop. Interior planes are computed on a compute stream while the halo exchange proceeds on the host/halo stream, so communication is fully overlapped; CUDA events sequence the boundary-plane kernels after the exchange.\n- **OpenMP** — parallelizes the remaining host-side O(N) work (NaN/Inf + min/max scan of the gathered field).\n- **CMakeLists.txt** — CUDA language enabled, `native` architecture, `find_package(MPI/OpenMP)`, same executable name `cahn_hilliard`, no new dependencies.\n\n**Correctness**: output text, CLI, and exit codes are unchanged.
